The standing owner instruction at `:58-59` is unaffected.\n- **`.agents/skills/orkestrel-publish/references/wave.md`** — the sweep step.\n- **`PROPOSAL.md` at acceptance** — delete it. `.agents/orchestration.md` § Where campaign artifacts live is explicit: the narrative and every ruling live in the durable artifact that owns them, and a campaign document is evidence rather than a rule's home. What survives goes to `guides/scaffold.md` (the mechanism), the wave reference (the procedure), and the commit message (the decision and the 2026-08-26 measurement).\n\n## 10. Types-first order\n\nSee **Units**. The one ordering constraint outside the writer serialization: `npm run build` regenerates `host.json`, and `tests/config.test.ts:643-686` compares the committed file byte-identically against a fresh `stageInventory`. Every gate that reads the generated artifact runs after that regeneration.\n\n## Exit criterion\n\nThe campaign ends when each of these closes as implemented, repaired, retained, or intentionally excluded on evidence:\n\n- Membership expressed as `HOST_PATHS` plus `CANON_PATHS`, with staging widened and planning narrowed.\n- Pointer artifacts compiled, written by `new`, and repaired as stale in an existing target.\n- `dist/host` carries the canon set, `host.json` records it, and the committed inventory proof is green.\n- `catalog`, `audit`, `repair`, and `overwrite` each still act against a generated target.\n- The vendored policy suite green in scaffold and in a target whose canon trees are absent.\n- Guide, README, and wave procedure state what shipped; `PROPOSAL.md` disposed of.\n- Gate chain green; `scaffold` released; each target re-pinned, repaired, swept, and proven green.\n\n---\n\n# Alternatives\n\n**A. Pointer as a vendored `HOST_PATHS` member with `source` remapping.** Put the pointer bytes at `.agents/pointers/agents.md`, keep it in `HOST_PATHS`, and give the plan artifact `{ path: 'AGENTS.md', source: '.agents/pointers/agents.md' }`. `Materializer.#expand` and `#remap` (`:658-689`, `:766-777`) support this today, so it works.\n\nIt loses because `nameToHostArtifacts` currently maps one path to one artifact with no source, and this route requires a source-to-path table whose only two rows are the pointers — new data expressing a mapping the template mechanism does not need. It also puts a member in `HOST_PATHS` whose destination is not its target path, which falsifies that constant's own contract for every other reader. The template route uses each mechanism for what it is: a byte-copy of a real file is a host artifact, and generated prose scaffold owns is a template artifact.\n\n**B. `claudeMdExcludes` only.** `PROPOSAL.md:115-118` already ranks it a stopgap. Objectively it also fails on its own terms: it is per-environment configuration with no repository-side expression, its cloud behavior is unmeasured (`PROPOSAL.md:35`), and it removes no byte of drift. It stays available as a same-day mitigation while the release is prepared, and it is not the fix.\n\n---\n\n# Units\n\nOne writer at a time, in the main checkout, from a clean committed baseline. The Codex bench is dark, so every unit that would route to Sol runs on the Opus `implementer` with the substitution recorded.\n\n**U1 — Constants, predicate, and pointer templates**\nRole `implementer`, engine Opus 5 (recorded substitution for Sol).\nOwns `src/core/constants.ts`, `src/core/helpers.ts`, `src/core/templates.ts`, `src/core/compilers.ts`, `src/core/index.ts`.\nDepends on nothing.\nAccepts when: `CANON_PATHS` and `isCanonPath` are exported through the core barrel; `HOST_PATHS` no longer names any canon member; `blueprintToDocsArtifacts` emits content-owned template artifacts at `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md`; `npm run check` clean; `npm run test:src:core` green.\n\n**U2 — Staging and host resolution**\nRole `implementer`, engine Opus 5.\nOwns `src/server/helpers.ts`, `src/bin/CLI.ts`.\nDepends on U1.\nAccepts when: `stageHost` walks `HOST_PATHS` then `CANON_PATHS`; `filesToHost` retains floor bytes for a canon destination; `#host` excludes canon destinations from the upstream fetch; `npm run check` clean; `npm run test:src:server` and `test:src:bin` green.\n\n**U3 — Fixtures and server tests**\nRole `implementer`, engine Opus 5.\nOwns `tests/setupServer.ts`, `tests/src/server/**`, `tests/src/bin/**`, `tests/src/core/**`, `tests/distribution.test.ts`.\nDepends on U2.\nAccepts when: `HOST_DIRECTORY_PATHS` matches the surviving planned directory members and retains one member `createCheckout` leaves genuinely empty, so the empty-root case `#empty` exists for keeps a fixture; the packed-path expansion covers `dist/host` including canon storage names; each named project green.\n\n**U4 — Vendored policy re-scope**\nRole `implementer`, engine Opus 5.\nOwns `tests/policy.test.ts`.\nOff-limits: `tests/setupPolicy.ts` (no inspector change is required, and touching it widens the blast radius across the fleet).\nDepends on U1.\nAccepts when: the skill-family case asserts the family against a direct read of its root; the path-population case names `.claude/settings.json`; `npm run test:policy` green in this checkout; the same file green when run against a scratch workspace with `.agents/skills`, `.claude/skills`, and `.claude/rules` absent and `.claude/settings.json` present.\n\n**U5 — Regenerate the inventory**\nRole `builder`, engine Sonnet.\nOwns `host.json`.\nDepends on U1, U2.\nAccepts when: `npm run build` completes and `npm run test:config` is green with `host.json` byte-identical to a fresh `stageInventory`.\n\n**U6 — Documentation and parity**\nRole `implementer`, engine Opus 5 (subjective: this is documentation voice).\nOwns `guides/scaffold.md`, `README.md`, `PROPOSAL.md` (deletion).\nDepends on U1, U2, U5.\nAccepts when: `npm run test:guides` green; no `README.md` sentence describes an instruction file as vendored-into-target data; the guide states the dot-stripped fallback paths the pointer names.\n\n**U7 — Wave procedure**\nRole `builder`, engine Sonnet.\nOwns `.agents/skills/orkestrel-publish/references/wave.md`.\nDepends on U6.\nAccepts when: the per-repo visit names re-pin, `repair`, the exact `git rm -r` path list, and the proof that the removal landed; `npm run test:policy` green (the skill inventory rules bind this file's directory).\n\n**U8 — Gates**\nRole `verifier`, engine Sonnet.\nOwns nothing.\nDepends on U1–U7.\nAccepts when: `format:check`, `lint:check`, `check`, `build`, `test` each run and their real output is reported.\n\n---\n\n# Tensions\n\nNamed for the other lane, each with the reasoning that produced it so the Orchestrator can weigh a different answer.\n\n1. **`.claude/agents` and `.codex/agents` stay in targets, against `PROPOSAL.md:81`.** I keep them because `catalog` throws without the catalog file, because they are the only thing keeping foreign detection and `remove` from going permanently inert in every generated workspace, and because role files cost zero memory bytes. The one-canonical-copy principle argues the other way, and the counter-move exists: plan `.claude/agents/orkestrel.md` alone as a file member, accept that `overwrite` becomes a no-op, and document that as a limit.\n2. **The `orkestrel-falsify` membership assertion is dropped rather than re-homed.** I judged the alternatives worse: a new cross-cutting proof violates the fixed table, and a repository-identity branch inside a vendored file creates a path nobody runs.\n3. **The pointer is a template rather than a vendored file.** Both work. I chose by which mechanism the artifact *is*, not by which is fewer lines.\n4. **The orphan sweep is documented, not mechanized.** A subjective reading might hold that a fleet-scale deletion nobody can forget is worth a verb. I hold that a one-release event does not earn permanent public API.\n5. **The pointer prose spells out the dot-stripped fallback paths and explains why they differ.** It reads awkwardly. The alternative — changing `pathToStorage` so the fallback paths match the source paths — is refused: it would move every storage name in `host.json` and in every published `dist/host`, for prose comfort.\n6. **A target can no longer carry harness-specific notes of its own.** `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md` are content-owned, so `repair` rewrites them. This is the status quo today, so it is not a regression, but the migration is the moment to decide whether it should be.\n\n---\n\n# Risks\n\n**R1 — Skills are unreachable in a target session.** With `.agents/skills` and `.claude/skills` out of targets, whether a session opened in a target with `--add-dir ../scaffold` can invoke a skill is undocumented. *Settled by:* opening a session in a migrated target with scaffold added and listing the available skills. If it cannot, the operating rule becomes \"open the session at the scaffold checkout and add targets\", which is the direction `PROPOSAL.md:65-67` already assumes. My design removes the same risk for roles by keeping `.claude/agents` and `.codex/agents`.\n\n**R2 — The sweep is load-bearing and manual.** A target that is re-pinned and repaired but not swept keeps `.claude/rules/writing.md`, which still auto-loads 8,220 bytes because it has no `paths:` frontmatter. The measured win is not realized for that repository. *Settled by:* after each visit, an empty listing of the removed paths and a clean `git status`, then the cloud context-panel reading `PROPOSAL.md:128-129` asks for.\n\n**R3 — Cloud memory behavior for `.claude/rules` per attached root.** The brief scopes this out of the code change, and I depend on it only for sizing the win, never for correctness: the pointer removes `CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.md`, and the `@` import regardless of how rules load. *Settled by:* the before-and-after context panel.\n\n**R4 — The live overlay's fetch set.** Without the `#host` and `filesToHost` edits, every `audit` and `repair` in every target fetches the canon set from upstream. With them, canon bytes come only from the installed floor and are never refreshed live — correct, because no verb writes them. *Settled by:* asserting the destination list `#host` passes to `Upstream.read` excludes every canon path, with a negative control that a planned path is still present.\n\n**R5 — Deletion and foreign-detection scope shrinks.** After the change, `remove` reaches files under `.claude/agents`, `.codex/agents`, and `.cursor/rules` only. A target that re-creates `.agents/` or `.claude/rules` is not told. *Settled by:* deciding it is acceptable and stating it in `guides/scaffold.md` § Integrity as what `overwrite` does and does not reach — not by leaving the guide's current wording, which would then be false.\n\n**R6 — The empty-root case loses its production subject.** `.claude/skills` is the one declared directory no entry sits beneath (`tests/setupServer.ts:1091-1093`), and it leaves. `#empty` and the empty-directory write path keep their fixture coverage only if U3 deliberately re-picks a root the fixture leaves empty. *Settled by:* a fixture assertion naming the chosen root, with the existing empty-root write test still green.\n\n**R7 — `.codex/config.toml` and `.cursor/rules/orchestration.mdc` name paths that no longer resolve in a target.** No test breaks; the prose does. *Settled by:* the pointer's path-resolution paragraph, and by reading those two files against a migrated target before release rather than assuming the paragraph covers them."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7863,"cache_read_input_tokens":187988,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7863,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CeSQnTW8R4jN6sHQGgBrx","attributionAgent":"planner","type":"assistant","uuid":"191a5419-d151-4a9d-a0ba-522083116d4e","timestamp":"2026-08-27T01:50:25.922Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"remote","cwd":"/home/user/scaffold","sessionId":"dc70cba3-3e2a-551f-9e05-1da04bbbf122","version":"2.1.247","gitBranch":"claude/scaffold-proposal-impl-nabmm9"}
